MADRAS.





mukavurai



acōkaṉatu cāsaṉaṅkaḷ ulaka carittirattilēyē oru putumai eṉalām. āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kalveṭṭukkaḷum vēṟu palavita purātaṉa likitaṅkaḷum namakku ippuviyiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷiliruntu kiṭaittirukkiṉṟaṉa ; āyiṉum taṉ pirajaikaḷiṉ kṣēmattiṉ poruṭṭu, tarmōpatēcaṅkaḷai eḻutivaitta aracaṉai nām vēṟeṅkuṅ kaṇṭilēm. inta likitaṅkaḷ intiya carittirattil mikavum ēṟṟamuṭaiya tastāvējukaḷ, mutalāvatu, ivaṟṟaiviṭap paḻaimaiyāṉa likitaṅkaḷ anēkamāka intiyāviṟ kiṭaiyā. ivaṟṟiṉ karuttai aṟiyum poruṭṭuc ceyyappaṭṭa ārāyccikaḷ intiyāviṟ cilācāsaṉa ārāyccikkut toṭakkamākum. iraṇṭāvatu, ivaṟṟiṟ kāṇappaṭum lipi (eḻuttu) nam nāṭṭil taṟkālam upayōkap paṭuttappaṭum pala eḻuttukkaḷiṉ ātiyai viḷakkukiṟatu. mūṉṟāvatu, pautta mata carittirattai viḷakka iccāsaṉaṅkaḷ iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa, nāṉkāvatu, inta likitaṅkaḷiluḷḷa pāṣai ki. mu. mūṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭaintiyāvil pēcappaṭṭu vanta moḻiyāṉatu vaṭamoḻiyōṭum pirākruta naṭaikaḷōṭum evvita oṟṟumai vēṟṟumaikaḷai uṭaiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa eṉpatai viḷakkum. ilakkiyamākak karutumiṭattum acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷ mikac cirēṣṭamāṉavaikaḷē. ivaṟṟiṟ peruntaṉmaiyuṭaiya ōr aracaṉ itayattaik kāṇkiṟōm ; avaṉuṭaiya uḷḷattiṉ uyarvum kaṉivum evaraiyum viyappaṭaiyac ceyyumeṉpatiṟ cantēkamillai.

icciṟu pustakattai vācikkum aṉpar yāvarum intiya carittirattaippaṟṟiya ārāyccikaḷiṟ piriyam koḷḷat tūṇṭappaṭalām. ēṉeṉṟāl, carittirattiṉ mūlātāramāyuḷḷa tastāvējukaḷait tāmē paṭittu viṣayaṅkaḷai āyvatu mikavum iṉpamāṉa kāriyamāyirukkavēṇṭum. evvaḷavu virivāka eḻutappaṭṭirukkum carittirap pustakaṅkaḷaip paṭippatum itaṟku nikarākātu.



ippustakattil avatārikai caṟṟu virivāka amaintiruppatu oru kuṟaiyākāteṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ. acōkaṉ caritai, āṭcimuṟai, akkālattup perumaiyait terivikkum aṟikuṟikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai vivarippatu iccāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ mukkiyattai uṇaruvataṟku avaciyamāṉatāl avatārikaiyil ivviṣayaṅkaḷ viḷakkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.

cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppu, iṅkilīṣ peyarppukkaḷai maṭṭum aṉusarittirāmal mūlattōṭum oppiṭṭuc cīrtiruttappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. palavita iyaṟkai vittiyācaṅkaḷ niṟaintiruntapōtum intiyāvil nuṭpamāṉa oṟṟumai uḷḷūṟap paraviyiruppatupōla, intiya moḻikaḷukkum neruṅkiya uṟavu. irukkiṉṟatu. utāraṇamāka, samskirutam pirākirutam mūlamāka vanta coṟkaḷ pala pāṣaikaḷukkum potuvāka uḷḷaṉa; iḵtaṉṟi, vācakarītiyilum aṇiyilum oṟṟumai kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ātiyil innāṭṭup pāṣaiyoṉṟil eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyattiṟku maṟṟoru intiyamoḻiyiṟ ceyyappaṭum peyarppu iṅkilīṣ moḻipeyarppaiviṭa mēṉmaiyāyiruppatu iyalpaṉṟō? ākaiyāl acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ tamiḻ moḻi peyarppu iṅkilīṣ moḻipeyarppaiviṭa mūlattiṉ pōkkai viḷakka ēṟṟatāyirukkavēṇṭum. appaṭiyillāviṭṭāl aḵtu āciriyaṉ kuṟṟamēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu; karuttu viḷaṅkuvataṟkuc ciṟitum kaṣṭamiṉṟiyum cilacamayam taṉṉai aṟiyāmalum mūlattiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ it tamiḻuraiyil vantiruppatu ivvapippirāyattukkuc cāṉṟākum.

ivvita pirayattaṉaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭaitta utavikaḷaik kūṟuvatu marapu; iḵtu eṉatu piriyamāṉa kaṭamaiyākavumirukkiṉṟatu. ceṉṉai carvakalācālaiyiṉ intiya carittira āciriyarākiya ṭākṭar es. kiruṣṇasvāmi aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ puttakattiṉ eḻuttup piratiyaip paricōtittuc cila kuṟṟaṅkuṟaikaḷait tīrttuḷḷārkaḷ. tiruvaṉantapuram kiranta paripālaṉālayattaic cērnta ma-⁰⁰-⁰⁰-srī hariharacāstirikaḷ eṉakku mutalil im muyaṟcikku ūkkattaik koṭuttār. maṟṟum pala naṇparkaḷ itaṉ eḻuttuppiratiyaiyō allatu accup piratiyaiyō vācittup pala kuṟaikaḷait tīrttirukkiṉṟaṉar. iṅkilīṣil acōkaṉaippaṟṟiya viyācaṅkaḷum, pustakaṅkaḷum, vicēṣamāka srī. viṉseṇṭ smittiṉ "acōkaṉ" eṉṟa pustakamum srī pūlariṉ cāsaṉa moḻipeyarppum eṉakku mikunta upayōkamāyiruntaṉa. intiyaṉ āṉṭikkuvari mutaliya ārāyccip pattirikaikaḷil acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷil varum coṟkaḷ, coṟṟoṭarkaḷ, vākkiyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ karuttaip paṟṟip pala vātaṅkaḷ vantuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟaiyum nāṉ iyaṉṟavāṟu upayōkappaṭutti yirukkiṟēṉ.

70-m pakkattiluḷḷa cittiramum 150-m pakkattiluḷḷa kalveṭṭum "eppikrāpiyā intikkā" VIII, V m vālyuṅkaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭavai.

cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ mūlattaiyum tēvanākara eḻuttilō kiranta eḻuttilō eḻuti ittuṭaṉ piracurañ ceyyavēṇṭumeṉṟa eṉ viruppam viraivil kaikūṭumeṉṟu niṉaikkiṟēṉ. moḻi peyarppai mūlattōṭu oppiṭac caukariyappaṭumāṟu ovvoru cāsaṉamum mūlattil ittaṉai vākkiyaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu ataṉ kīḻuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷiṟ kāṭṭap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa, acōka likitaṅkaḷ 'eppikrāpiyā intikkā' I, II, III, V, VIII vālyuṅkaḷilum intiyaṉ āṉṭikkuvari. XIX, XX, XXII vālyuṅkaḷilum āṅkila eḻuttiṟ piracuramāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. kalkattā yūṉivarviṭṭiyiṉ patippu iccāsaṉaṅkaḷai kaikku aṭakkamāka oru ciṟu puttakamākkiyirukkiṟatu. ivaṟṟil āṅkila eḻuttil eḻutip piracurañ ceyyappaṭṭirukkum mūlattai ittamiḻ moḻipeyarppuṭaṉum acciṭa uttēcikkiṉṟēṉ.





poruḷaṭakkam





* * *



avatārikai



I intiyāviṉ pūrvacarittira ārāycci

carittira aṟiviṉ vaḷarcci. pūrva intiya carittirattiṉ ātāraṅkaḷ. acōkaṉatu cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ karuttai viḷakkiya ārāyccikaḷ. ... 1—8

II. acōkaṉ caritai:-

makatatēcattiṉ perumai. pintucāraṉ. acōkaṉaip paṟṟi aitihyaṅkaḷiliruntu kiṭaikkum vivaraṅkaḷ. aracaṉ paṭṭāpiṣēkam, kaliṅkayuttam. pauttamatattiṉ sparcam. tīrtta yāttiraikaḷ. tuṟavi aracu naṭattiya vintai. tarmappiracāram ceytal. veku tūrattuḷḷa aintu aracaruṭaṉ uṟavu. itiliruntu kiṭaikkum "sama kālattuvam." kuṇaticai ulakam kuṭaticai ulakattaic cantittatu. ājīvakarkaḷukkuk kukaikaḷ ceytal. stampacāsaṉaṅkaḷaip piracurañ ceytal. pautta makāsapai. acōkaṉ kuṇam. acōkaṉukkuppiṉ vanta mōriya aracar. ... 8—30

III. acōkaṉ tarmam:—

acōkaṉ kālattil pautta matattil ēṟpaṭṭa māṟutalkaḷ. pautta caṅka capaikaḷ. mūṉṟāvatu caṅka capai. saṉmārkka pōtaṉaiyē pautta matattiṉ sāram eṉka. mūṭa nampikkaikaḷaik kaṇṭittal. jīva imsai nivāraṇam. cuvarkkattil nampikkai. acōkaṉukkup piṉ pautta matattil ēṟpaṭṭa māṟutalkaḷ. ... 31-43

IV. acōkaṉ aracāṭci:—

acōkaṉ turaittaṉam muṉṉiruntataṉ toṭarcciyē. aracaṉatu putu nōkkaṅkaḷ. camarasapāvam. kṣēmāpivirttikkāṉa putuēṟpāṭukaḷ. vivacāyam, kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ, cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum atikārikaḷ. aṉusamyāṉam. acōka ēkātipattiyattiṉ virivu. ...... 43-58

V. akkālattup paḻamporuḷkaḷ:—

ivaṟṟilum paḻamaiyāṉavai intiyāvil kiṭaiyā. acōkaṉuṭaiya vēlaikaḷ. ivaṟṟiṉ taṟkāla aṟikuṟikaḷ. stūpaṅkaḷ. sāñcistūpam. parkhūt stūpam, kayai stūpam, stūpaṅkaḷil uṇṭāṉa māṟutalkaḷ. kukaikaḷ. stampaṅkaḷ. ivaṟṟiṉ ciṟpattiṟamai. intac ciṟpattaip paṟṟiya matippu. .... 58-69

VI. acōka eḻuttu:—

iruvita purātaṉa lipikaḷ. pirāmmi lipi intiya eḻuttukkaḷukku mūlātāram. intiya pāṣaikaḷukku lipi ēṟpaṭṭa vitam. karōṣṭi lipi. ... 69-73

VII. piriyatarci aracaṉ kalveṭṭukkaḷ:—

ivaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭu. ivaṟṟiṉ taṉmai. vācakarīti. cācaṉaṅkaḷ allāta likitaṅkaḷ. likitaṅkaḷiṉ pirivu. acōka likitaṅkaḷiṉ pāṣai. ilakkaṇak kuṟippu. .... 73-80

acōkaṉuṭaiya cāsaṉaṅkaḷ



avatārikai

1. intiyāviṉ pūrva carittira ārāycci



intiyāviṉ pūrva carittiramāṉatu māṉiṭa jātiyiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattai viḷakkuvataṟku mika ariya cātaṉamām. intiya carittirattiluḷḷa araca paramparaiyil acōkaṉ ōr naṭunāyakamāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. ivaṉuṭaiya viṉōtamāṉa cāsaṉaṅkaḷaip paṭittuṇara ivvaracaṉaippaṟṟiya ellā viṣayaṅkaḷaiyum muṉṉuraiyākak kūṟuvatu avaciyameṉak karutukiṟōm.

nūṟṟaimpatu varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉvaraiyum mēṉāṭukaḷil intiyāviṉ pūrvika carittirattaippaṟṟi mikak kuṟaivāṉa aṟivutāṉ iruntatu. appōtu mukammatiyar varukaikku muṉṉuḷḷa kālattiṉ carittirattai yārum terintu koḷḷavillai, ki. pi. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṭivil, mēl nāṭṭār samskiruta ilakkiyam eṉṟa makācamuttirattiṉ āḻattaiyum virivaiyum uṇara ārampittaṉar. āyiṉum, ulakattiṉ carittirattil intiya carittiramāṉatu mikavum mukkiyamāṉa pākameṉpatu oruvarukkum pulappaṭavillai. nam nāṭṭu vittuvāṉkaḷō, carittiram eṉṟa viṣayattai āḻnta paṭippum ārāycciyum avaciyamāṉa oru cās tiramākak karutavillai. mēṉāṭṭārō, tamatu pirakiruti cāstirat tērcciyilum nākarikattiṉ mēṉmaiyilum tāmē mayaṅkit taṅkaḷukkup puviyil evarum nikarillai yeṉṟum maṟṟa jātiyāriṉ carittiram ulakattiṉ muṟpōkkukku avaciyamillaiyeṉṟum niṉaittu iṟu māppaṭaintiruntaṉar. ōr putu tiṉam uṇṭāvatu pōla meḷḷa meḷḷa iruḷ akaṉṟu ippōtu oru putu uṇarcci paravivarukiṟatu, inta nūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukkuk kiṭaiyil intiyāviṉ carittiram ulakattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattukku evvaḷavu avaciyamāṉatu eṉpatai aṟiñar uṇarntu varukiṉṟaṉar.

pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil intiyāviṉ ﻿

carittira aṟiviṉ

vaḷarcci

﻿nākarikattaip paṟṟiya ārāycci toṭaṅkiṟṟu. ataṉ piṟakum pala varuṣaṅkaḷukku nam tēcattiṉ pūrvacarittiram uḷḷē piravēcikka vaḻiyillāta peruṅ kāṭāyiruntatu. virivāṉa purāṇak kataikaḷum tarma cāstiraṅkaḷum tattuva vētāntak kirantaṅkaḷum aitihyaṅkaḷu mallāmal, kālanirṇayamuḷḷa vivaraṅkaḷ ic carittirattil kāṇappaṭavillai. toṭakkam, muṭivu, ellai mutaliya yātoru aḷavumillāmalum kāla varaiyaṟai yillāmalum tokukkappaṭṭa pustakaṅkaḷ intiyāviṉ carittiramāy viḷaṅkiṉa. ippaṭippaṭṭa kāṭṭiṉūṭē nuḻaintu cella namatu "purātaṉavastu cāstirikaḷ” ōr vaḻiyai amaittirukkiṟārkaḷ.



purātaṉa vastu cāstiram eṉṟāl eṉṉa? paṇṭaikkāla nilaimaiyiṉ aṟikuṟiyāka ittēcam muḻumaiyilum parantu kiṭakkum iṭinta kōyilkaḷ, pāḻāṉa nakaraṅkaḷ, iṉṉum citaiyāta maṟṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷ, putaiyalāy namakku akappaṭum paṟpalavita nāṇayaṅkaḷ, ceppuppaṭṭayaṅkaḷ, kalveṭṭukaḷ, stampaṅkaḷ, piratimaikaḷ mutaliya vastukkaḷai “purātaṉa vastu”[1] eṉṟu nām iṅku kūṟuvōm. ivaṟṟiliruntu nam ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāka namakkuk kiṭaittirukkum aṟivē purātaṉa vastu cāstiram[2].

pūrva intiya

carittirattiṉ

ātāraṅkaḷpūrvika intiya carittirattiṉ ātāraṅkaḷai mūṉṟu vakaiyākap pirikkalām.

mutalāvatu, aitihyam. atāvatu intiyāviluḷḷa ellāvita kirantaṅkaḷilum aṭaṅkiya vivaraṅkaḷ. curuti, smiruti, vētāṅkaṅkaḷ, itikāsam, purāṇam, eṉṟa palavakai kirantaṅkaḷaiyum ippirivil nām aṭakkukiṟōm. intak kirantaṅkaḷaippōlavē ivaṟṟil aṭaṅkiya camācāraṅkaḷum aḷavaṟṟavai.

iraṇṭāvatu, anniya nāṭṭār intiyāvaippaṟṟit tam tam pāṣaikaḷil eḻuti vaittuḷḷa vivaraṅkaḷ alaksāntaruṭaṉum ataṟkup piṉṉum vanta yavaṉa tūtarkaḷum yavaṉa tēcāntarikaḷum pāhiyaṉ, yuvaṉcuvaṅ, itsiṅ mutaliya cīṉa tēcāntarikaḷum, tāṅkaḷ it tēcattil kaṇṭatum kēṭṭatumāṉa ceytikaḷai eḻutivaittirukkiṟārkaḷ. avai yāvum akappaṭavillaiyāyiṉum kiṭaittavaraiyilum iv vātāraṅkaḷ kāla nirṇayattukku mika mukkiyamāṉavai. mūṉṟāvatu, purātaṉa vastucāstiram. itil mūṉṟu mukkiyamāṉa uṭpirivukaḷ uṇṭu. (i) paḻaiya kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷaic cōtaṉai ceyvatiliruntum ciṟpaṅkaḷil kālattukkut takkapaṭi ēṟpaṭum vittiyācaṅkaḷaik kavaṉippatiliruntum uṇṭākum aṟivu. (Architecture and Sculpture). (ii) pāṟaiyilum kōyilkaḷiṉ kaṟcuvarilum ceppup paṭṭayaṅkaḷilum varaintu kāṇappaṭum vikitaṅkaḷaip paṭippataṉāl kiṭaikkum aṟivu. (Epigraphy). (iii) paḻaiyakālattu nāṇayaṅkaḷaip paricōtaṉai ceyvataṉ payaṉāka uṇṭākum aṟivu (Numismatics). mutalāvatu pirivaic cērnta aitihyam eṉṟa ātārattiliruntu namakkuk kiṭaittuḷḷa vivaraṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟavaiyāṉālum avaṟṟai oḻuṅku paṇṇik kālattiṉpaṭi varicaippaṭuttiyatu, purātaṉa vastu ārāycciyiṉ utaviyum anniya nāṭṭār eḻuti vaittuḷḷa vivaraṇaṅkaḷiṉ utaviyumē. itaṉṟi ikkāriyam acāttiyamāyiruntirukkum. purātaṉa vastu cāstirattiṉ utavi pūrvika intiyāviṉ carittirattukku evvaḷavu mukkiya ātāramākumeṉṟu uṇara acōkaṉatu cāsaṉaṅkaḷ takka cāṉṟākum.

acōkaṉ eṉṟa peyaruṭaiya ōr aracaṉ intiyāvil ﻿

acōkaṉatu

cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ

karuttai viḷakkiya

ārāyccikaḷ

﻿āṇṭatāka intukkaḷukku nūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ varaiyum koñcamum teriyāmaliruntatu. ēṟakkuṟaiya āyira varuṣaṅkaḷāka avaṉ peyarē intiyariṉ ñāpakattiliruntu māyntu pōyiruntatu. vaṭa intiyāviṉ pala pākaṅkaḷil niṉṟirunta acōka stampaṅkaḷai muṉ kālattilirunta rākṣacariṉ vēlaiyeṉṟu nam jaṉaṅkaḷ kūṟivantaṉar. stampaṅkaḷilum pāṟaikaḷilum eḻutappaṭṭirunta likitaṅkaḷukku oruvarukkum poruḷ teriyavillai. ippaṭi irukkaiyil 1834ām ௵l īsṭu intiyāk 'kampaṉiyāriṉ nāṇayap paricōtaṉai atikāriyākiya jēms priṉsep eṉpavarukku apūrvamāṉa paḻaiya nāṇayaṅkaḷ pala kiṭaittaṉa. inta nāṇayaṅkaḷiṉ oru pakkattil krīk lipiyilum maṟṟap pakkattil appōtu intiyāvil vaḻaṅkivanta iruvakai lipi oṉṟilum vākkiyaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ippaṭippaṭṭa nāṇayaṅkaḷai tvipāṣai nāṇayaṅkaḷ[3] eṉṟu nām collalām. inta nāṇayaṅkaḷ intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟkup pākattil ki. mu. 200 mutal ki. pi. 100 varaiyum āṇṭuvanta krīk allatu yavaṉa aracarkaḷāl aṭikkappaṭṭavai. krīk pāṣaiyilum krīk lipiyilum inta nāṇayaṅkaḷaic ceyta aracarkaḷiṉ peyarum virutukaḷum ivaṟṟiṉ oru pakkattil eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa. airōppiya vittuvāṉ' āṉatiṉāl jēms priṉseppukku krīkkil eḻutappaṭṭirukkum vivaraṅkaḷai vācittu aṟintukoḷvatu eḷitāyiruntatu. nāṇayaṅkaḷiṉ maṟupakkattil eḻutiyiruppatu krīkkil eḻutappaṭṭa vivaraṅkaḷēyeṉṟu avar ūkittār. aṟiyappaṭāta iruvakai lipikaḷiṉ ovvoru eḻuttaiyum maṟupakkattiluḷḷa krīk lipiyōṭu ottuppārttu avveḻuttukkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa olikaḷaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭum paṭṭikai oṉṟai priṉsep tayārceytār. stampaṅkaḷilum pāṟaikaḷilum veṭṭappaṭṭirunta likitaṅkaḷil tvipāṣai nāṇayaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum intiya lipikaḷē upayōkap paṭuttappaṭṭiruppatu piṉpu teriyavantatu. paḻaiya kalveṭṭukkaḷai vācittu aṟintukoḷḷap piriṉceppiṉ paṭṭikai mikunta pirayōjaṉamāyiṟṟu. āyiṉum likitaṅkaḷiṉ karuttu viḷaṅkuvatu atikak kaṣṭamāyiruntatu.

acōkaṉatu cāsaṉaṅkaḷaippaṭikka muyaṉṟavaril mutalvarum inta priṉsep tāṉ. āṅkilamoḻiyil piracittipeṟṟa “rājaputtira carittiram” eḻutiya karṉal ṭāṭ (Colonel Tod) ivaraip piṉpaṟṟi kirnār eṉṟa iṭattiluḷḷa likitaṅkaḷaippaṭikka muyaṉṟār. mutalil inta likitaṅkaḷiṉ karuttu oruvarukkum teṉpaṭavillai. piyatasi eṉṟa ōr aracaṉāl eḻutappaṭṭa likitaṅkaḷ ivai eṉṟu maṭṭum teriyavantatu. āṉāl pala camucayaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. cila likitaṅkaḷil, tēvāṉām - priyaṉ (= tēvarkaḷukkup piriyamāṉavaṉ) eṉṟu aracaṉiṉ virutu peyarāka upayōkikkappaṭṭiruntatu. maṟṟa likitaṅkaḷil piyatasi eṉṟa peyarmaṭṭuntāṉ kāṇappaṭṭatu. vēṟu likitaṅkaḷil piyatasi eṉṟa peyarukku ‘tēvāṉāmpiriyaṉ’ eṉṟa patam virutāka upayōkappaṭṭiruntatu. iv vivaraṅkaḷiliruntu pala kēḷvikaḷ piṟantaṉa: intap piyatasi evar? piyatasi oruvarā palarā? piyatasi eṉṟa peyar ciṟappuppeyarā? allatu potuppeyarā? piyatasiyum tēvāṉāmpiriyamṉum vevvēṟu maṉitarkaḷā, allatu iraṇṭum orē maṉitaṉiṉ peyarā? ippaṭippaṭṭa kēḷvikaḷukkuk cila varuṣaṅkaḷāka viṭai kiṭaikkavillai.

piṟaku purātaṉavastu cāstirikaḷum maṟṟuḷḷa vāṉkaḷum pala tuṟaikaḷil ārāyccivēlaiyai toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. airōppiyaruḷ srī. kaṉṉiṅhām (Cunningham) parjes, (Bargess) pūlar (Bihler) seṉārṭ, (Senart) porṉāp (Burnouf) eṉpōrum, nammavaruḷ srī. rāmakiruṣṇa pantarkkar, pakavaṉlāl intirājī, rājēntiralāl mittirar, eṉṟa vitvāṉkaḷum acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ karuttai viḷakkuvataṟkup pirayācappaṭṭārkaḷ. paḻaiya lipikaḷ ellām vācikkappaṭṭaṉa ; purātaṉa vastukkaḷ cōtaṉai ceyyappaṭṭuk kāppāṟṟappaṭṭaṉa; likitaṅkaḷ cariyāṉa nakalkaḷuṭaṉum moḻipeyarppukaḷuṭaṉum viyākkiyāṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉum piracurañ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa ; hintu, jeyiṉa pautta mataṅkaḷaiccārnta kirantaṅkaḷ vivēcaṉa puttiyuṭaṉ paṭikkappaṭṭamaiyāl avaṟṟiliruntu pūrvika carittirattukkup pala ātāraṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa ; acōkaṉ eṉṟa peyaruṭaiya aracaṉ oru kālattil intiyā muḻuvataiyum āṇṭu vantavaṉeṉṟum avaṉukkup piyatasi eṉṟa virutu iruntateṉṟum pauttak kirantaṅkaḷiliruntu teriyavantatu. pauttarkaḷatu teyva pāṣaiyākiya pālikkum acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷiṉ pāṣaikkumuḷḷa neruṅkiya campantam veḷiyāyiṟṟu. likitaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭirukkum piyatasi, tēvāṉām piriyaṉ, tēvāṉāmpiriyaṉāṉa piyatasi, ellām orē maṉitaṉaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, peyaril eṉṉa māṟupā ṭiruntapōtilum inta likitaṅkaḷ vācakarītiyil orē maṉitaṉiṉ vākkiyaṅkaḷeṉṟu niccayamāyc collakkūṭiya oṟṟumaiyaiyuṭaiyaṉa veṉṟum, ellōrum aṟiyalāyiṉar. kaṭaiciyāka, ki. pi. 1915m ௵l reyccūrukku arukiluḷḷa māski eṉṟa ūril kiṭaitta acōkak kalveṭṭil acōkaṉ eṉṟa peyarē varaintiruppatu teriyavantatum inta likitaṅkaḷai eḻutiyavaṉ evaṉ eṉṟa caṅkai muṟṟilum nīṅki viṭṭatu.



↑ † Antiquities purātaṉa vastukkaḷ.

↑ ★ Archaeology

↑ Bi-lingual coins





kuṟippukaḷ





II. acōkaṉiṉ caritai



ki. mu. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil makatatēcam vaṭa ﻿

makatatēcattiṉ

perumai.

﻿intiyāviluḷḷa tēcaṅkaḷil mutaṉmaiyai aṭaintiruntatu. in nāṭṭai āṇṭuvanta nantar eṉṟa aracarkaḷ iḻivu kulattōrākak karutappaṭṭa pōtilum parākramacālikaḷākavum yuttacaiṉṉiyaṅkaḷilum taḷavāṭaṅkaḷilum valimai mikuntavarākavumiruntaṉar. akanāṉūṟu eṉṟa paṇṭait tamiḻ nūliluḷḷa oru pāṭṭil iv vamicattiṉar “palpukaḻ niṟainta velpōr nantar” eṉṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭṭiruttalai iṅku kuṟippiṭuvatu takutiyākum. inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil, atāvatu cumār ki mu. 325m ௵l, makatanāṭṭil oru puraṭci ēṟpaṭṭatu. cāṇakkiyaṉ, viṣṇukuptaṉ, kauṭalyaṉ, kauṭilyaṉ eṉṟa peyarkaḷaiyuṭaiya ōr pirāmaṇa mantiriyiṉ cūḻccikaḷ kāraṇamāka nantar aracurimaiyai iḻakkavum cantirakuptaṉ eṉṟa mauriyavamcattiṉaṉ aracaṉākavum nērntaṉa. cantirakuptaṉ ellā vaṭa intiya rājyaṅkaḷaiyum kaippaṟṟi yāvaṟṟaiyum orē kuṭaiyiṉ kīḻk koṇṭuvantāṉ. ataṟku muṉ ittakaiya vicālamāṉa ēkātipattiyam intiyāvil ēṟpaṭavillaiyeṉṟu nām collalām. cantirakuptaṉiṉ varalāṟṟai viritturaippatu nam viṣayattukkup puṟamāyiṉum kauṭalyaṉāl eḻutappaṭṭa ‘arttacāstiram’ eṉṟa nūlaip paṟṟi iv vavatārikaiyiṉ maṟṟoru pākattil kūṟuvatu avaciyam. inta nūl ikkālattukku muṉṉum piṉṉumuḷḷa turaittaṉattiṉ amaippaiyum rājīya nōkkaṅkaḷaiyum naṉṟāka viḷakkukiṟatu.

jeyiṉa nūlkaḷil cantirakuptaṉ irupattaiyāṇṭu (ki,mu.325-301) aracu naṭattiya piṟaku ulakavāḻkkaiyil veṟuppaṭaintu pattirapāku eṉṟa jepiṉa muṉivaruṉ cīṭaṉāṉa tākavum am muṉivaruṭaṉ takkaṇam ceṉṟu paṉṉiriyāṇṭu tavampurintu kaṭaiciyil paṭṭiṉi viratamūlamākat tamatu uṭalaik kaḷaintatākavum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maicūr camstāṉattil cantirakiri eṉṟa iṭattil iv vaitihyaṅkaḷaik kuṟippiṭum kalveṭṭukaḷum ciṟpaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

mauriya vamcattu iraṇṭāvatu aracaṉāṉa pintucāraṉ ﻿

pintucāraṉ

﻿acōkaṉuṭaiya tantaiyātalāl ivaṉaippaṟṟip pauttak kirantaṅkaḷil cila rucikaramāṉa kataikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil uṇmaiyait tēṭi aṟivatu kaṣṭamāyirukkiṟatu. ivaṉ kālattil āṭci muṟaiyil vicēṣa māṟutalkaḷ ēṟpaṭavillaiyāyiṉum rājya kāriyaṅkaḷ oḻuṅkāka naṭantēṟiṉaveṉṟum, takṣiṇam mauriyariṉ ēkātipattiyattil aikkiya maṭaintatu ikkālattileṉṟum kūṟuvataṟku ātāraṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. cantirakupataṉ kālattil intiyāvukkum siriyā mutaliya kuṇa ticai nāṭukaḷukkum nē uṟavu ēṟpaṭṭatu; ivvuṟavu pintusāraṉ kālattilum nīṭittu vantatu. pintusāraṉ yavaṉaruṭaiya viññāṉa cāstiraṅkaḷai viyantu, ivaṟṟiṉ cūkṣumattait terintukoḷḷa, siriyā tēcattu aracaṉiṭam tērnta vittuvāṉkaḷait taṉakku aṉuppik koṭukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu kēṭṭatāka oru krīk varalāṟu irukkiṉṟatu. ivaṉ irupattēḻu varuṣaṅkaḷ aracupurintu piṉ maraṇamaṭaintāṉ (kālam, ki, mu. 301-273)

acōkaṉuṭaiya jīviya caritaikku avaṉatu cāsaṉaṅkaḷaiyum ﻿

acōkaṉaip

paṟṟi aitihyaṅ

kaḷiliruntu kiṭai

kkum vivaraṅkaḷ.

﻿purātaṉa ārāycciyāl teriyavantirukkum cila ceytikaḷaiyum nām mukkiya ātāramāy vaittuk koḷḷalām. acōkaṉaippaṟṟiya aitihyaṅkaḷ iraṇṭu cārpiliruntu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. (i) tivyāvatāṉam eṉṟa samskirutak kirantattiluḷḷa vaṭa intiya aitihyam. (ii) cilōṉ tīviluḷḷa pauttariṉ itikāsa kirantaṅkaḷākiya tīpavamcam, mahāvamcam eṉpaṉavaṟṟiliruntu kiṭaikkum cilōṉ aitihyam. inta iru cārpu aitihyaṅkaḷiliruntu acōkaṉaippaṟṟit teriyavarum ceytikaḷ ellām nām oppukkoḷḷak kūṭiyavaṉavā yirukkavillai. āṉāl avai muṟṟilum taḷḷattakkavaiyumalla. intukkaḷuṭaiya purāṇaṅkaḷil pūrvacarittira viṣayamāṉa pala ceytikaḷ aṭaṅkiyiruppiṉum aṅku acōkaṉuṭaiya aracāṭciyaippaṟṟi oru vivaramumillai. paṇṭaiya aracariṉ peyarkaḷukkiṭaiyil acōkaṉuṭaiya peyarum iruppatu oru mēṉmaiyāmō? patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappeṟṟa rājataraṅkiṇi eṉṟa kācmīra carittirattil acōkaṉuṭaiya cila varalāṟukaḷ kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. kācmīra nāṭṭiṉ pala kōyilkaḷum araṇkaḷum acōkaṉāl ākkappaṭṭavai yeṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. evvitamum, namakku pautta aitihyaṅkaḷai viṭa uṟutiyāṉa carittiracātaṉaṅkaḷillaiyeṉa nām oppukkoḷḷavēṇṭum. cāsaṉaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭāmalum pautta aitihyaṅkaḷiliruntu maṭṭum teriya varuvatumāṉa ceytikaḷukkup piṉ purātaṉa vastu ārāycciyiliruntu attāṭci akappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. itaṟku utāraṇam piṉ kūṟuvōm*[1]. āyiṉum cāsaṉaṅkaḷil collappaṭṭiruppataṟku virōtamāyirukkiṟa vivaraṅkaḷaiyum viṉōtak kaṟpaṉaikaḷeṉṟu veḷippaṭaiyāyt terikiṉṟa ceytikaḷaiyum nām uṇmaic carittiramāy oppukkoḷḷa muṭiyātu. ippaṭippaṭṭa kaṭṭukkataikaḷai iṅku virittuk kūṟuvatu aṉāvaciyameṉṟu namakkut tōṉṟukiṟatu.

acōkaṉiṉ iḷamaip paruvattaippaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ yāvum aitihyaṅkaḷil maṭṭumē uḷḷaṉa, acōkaṉ pintusāraṉiṉ mūttamakaṉalla. paṭṭattukku atika pāttiyataiyuṭaiya pala cakōtarar avaṉukkiruntaṉar. āyiṉum avaṉatu putti nuṭpamum cāmarttiyamum paṟṟi avaṉukku mukkiyamāṉa aracāṅka atikāraṅkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṉ mutalil takṣacilā eṉṟa mākāṇattiṉ atipaṉāyc cilakālam vēlai ceytapiṉ ujjeyiṉiyiṉ atipaṉāka niyamikkappaṭṭāṉ. pintusāraṉ tīēkaviyōkamāṉavuṭaṉ acōkaṉ pāṭalipurattai aṭaintu uparājaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭattuṭaṉ rājyattiṉ talaimaiyai vakikka ārampittāṉ, nāṉku varuṣaṅkaḷukkuppiṉ acōkaṉ paṭṭāpiṣēkam ceytu koṇṭāṉ.

ic ceytikaḷai nām nampalām; āṉāl acōkaṉ ciṅkātaṉattaik kaippaṟṟuvataṟkut toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatu cakōtararai avar maṉaivi makkaḷuṭaṉ kolai ceytāṉeṉṟum jaṉaṅkaḷaip pala vitattil kacakkip piḻintāṉeṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟa kataikaḷ uṇmaiyeṉṟu tōṉṟavillai. iv viṣayaṅkaḷ acōkaṉuṭaiya kuṇattaippaṟṟic cāsaṉaṅkaḷaip paṭippatiliruntu namakku ēṟpaṭum apippirāyattukku muṟṟilum muraṇpaṭukiṟatu. mēlum, perun tuṣṭaṉāṉa oruvaṉ tiṭīreṉṟu veku puṇṇiyavāṉāy viṭuvatu iyaṟkaiyalla. paktiyiṉ aṉukuṇattāl maṉitaṉiṉ uḷḷam cīraṭaiyumeṉpatu uṇmaiyē. acōkaṉum puttaruṭaiya cuvicēṣattai'c ceviyuṟṟatiṉāl pala paḻaiya tīya vaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum cupāvaṅkaḷaiyum tavirttut tarmātmāvāy māṟiyirukkalām.

āṉāl acōkaṉ mutalil mikak keṭṭavaṉākavum koṭuṅkōl aracaṉākavum iruntāṉ eṉpatum piṉ tiṭīreṉṟu periya muṉicirēṣṭaṉāṉāṉ eṉpatum iyaṟkaiyalla. carittira campavaṅkaḷ aitihyaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭum poḻutu avai ivvitam tirintupōkiṉṟaṉa, orē oru aracaṉ, mika mūrkkaṉākavum keṭṭavaṉākavum iruntavaṉ, tarmattaic ciravaṇam ceyta māttirattil saṉmārkattiṉ uruvē pōṉṟa paricutta maṉitaṉāy viṭṭāṉeṉṟa ceyti tarmattiṉ mēṉmaiyaiyum kuṇattaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭa, evvaḷavu nērttiyāṉa katai! tarmattaip pōtikka vēṇṭumeṉṟa uttēcattuṭaṉ tokukkappaṭṭa itikācaṅkaḷukku ik kataikaḷ evvaḷavu ucitamāyirukkiṉṟaṉa ! carittira campavaṅkaḷ tarmīya nōkkattuṭaṉ eḻutappaṭum purāṇaṅkaḷil ivvita māṟutal aṭaital iyaṟkaiyallavā?

ik kāraṇaṅkaḷpaṟṟi nammuṭaiya muṭivu eṉṉaveṉṟāl, acōkaṉ taṉ aracāṭciyai ārampitta poḻutu koṭuṅkōlaṉāyirāmal taṉatu vīraparākramattil nampikkaiyum vēṭṭaiyāṭutal mutaliya viyāyāmaṅkaḷil apilāṣaiyum ayal nāṭukaḷai jayikka vēṇṭumeṉṟa nōkkamumuṭaiya cātāraṇa aracaṉāyiruntāṉ. putta pakavāṉuṭaiya kāṟṟu avaṉmēl appōtu aṭikkātatāl ākāra viṣayamākavum teyva ārātaṉaikkākavum aracaṉuṭaiya araṇmaṉaiyil pirāṇivatai naṭantu vantatu[2]. acōkaṉuṭaiya patiṉāṉkāvatu paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil eḻutappaṭṭa aintām cāsaṉattiliruntu aracaṉuṭaiya cakōtara cakōtarikaḷ appōtu vāḻntu vantaṉareṉṟu nām aṟikiṟōm. ataṉāl acōkaṉ taṉatu uṟaviṉar yāvaraiyum kolaiceyta vivaram nampattakkatalla. pintusāraṉ iṟantatum acōkaṉ uṭaṉē aṭutta aracaṉāvataṟkut taṭai yiruntatu uṇmaiyāyirukkalām; cakōtararkaḷukkiṭaiyil caṇṭai caccaravukaḷ naṭantirukkalām; ivvaḷavu tāṉ nām aitihyattiliruntu kirakikkak kūṭiyatu. aracurimai kaipaṟṟiyataik kuṟittuḷḷa maṟṟa vivaraṅkaḷ uṇmaiyeṉat tōṉṟavillai.

acōkaṉ nālu varuṣam iḷavaracaṉāka aracāṭci ﻿

aracaṉiṉ

paṭṭāpiṣēkam

﻿naṭattiyapiṉ taṉakkup paṭṭāpiṣēkam ceytukoṇṭāṉ. ‘piyatasi muṭicūṭiya ittaṉai varuṣaṅkaḷāṉa piṉ’ eṉṟu pala cāsaṉaṅkaḷil aracaṉ kālattil naṭanta pala campavaṅkaḷukkuk kālam kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ataṉāl acōkaṉ karuttuppaṭit tāṉ paṭṭāpiṣēkam ceytukoṇṭa tiṉamē taṉatu āṭciyiṉ ārampatiṉam pōlum. at tiṉattil varuṣā varuṣam uṟcavam koṇṭāṭappaṭṭatu. appōtu maraṇa taṇṭaṉai vitikkappeṟṟa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷil cilarai viṭutalaiceyvatu vaḻakkam.[3]



acōkaṉuṭaiya jīviya caritaiyil kaliṅka yuttam ﻿

kaliṅka yuttam

﻿mika mukkiyamāṉa campavamākum. avaṉuṭaiya oṉpatāvatu paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil aracaṉ kaliṅka tēcattaik kaippaṟṟuvataṟkākap paṭaiyeṭuttāṉ. taṉatu ēkātipattiyam imayamalai mutal teṟkē pālāṟu varaiyum iṭaiviṭāmal toṭarntiruppataṟkuk kaliṅkar nāṭu maṭṭum vilakkāyiruntatu. ip piratēcam oru mūlaiyiliruntatiṉālum aṭarttiyāṉa kāṭukaḷāl cūḻappaṭṭiruntatiṉālum atu acōkaṉiṉ muṉṉōrāl ēkātipattiyattōṭu cērkkappaṭavillai. ik kuṟaiyait tīrppataṟku yuttam ārampikkappaṭṭatu. acōkaṉuṭaiya eṇṇamum kaikūṭiṟṟu. āṉāl avaṉ maṟṟa aracaraippōl taṉatu veṟṟiyil cantōṣamaṭaiyavillai. yuttattukku iyaṟkaiyāṉa kolaiyum koṭumaiyum kaṣṭaṅkaḷum avaṉ maṉattil tikilaic ceytaṉa. periyatōṣam ceytōmē yeṉṟu avaṉukku ēkkam piṭittatu. cuyamākavē acōkaṉ irakkamuḷḷavaṉ ; atōṭukūṭa itē camayattil aracaṉ pautta tarmattaik kēṭkavum nērntatu. avaṉ tāṉ paṭum paccāttāpattaip patiṉmūṉṟām cāsaṉattil kaṇṇuṅ kaṇṇīrumāy eḻutivaittirukkiṟāṉ. evvaḷavu periya lāpattukkāyiṉum iṉimēl oru pōtum yuttam ceyvatillaiyeṉṟu acōkaṉ piratikñai ceytāṉ. appiratikñai kāppāṟṟappaṭṭatu avaṉatu niṣkapaṭamāṉa cupāvattai viḷakkukiṟatu.

eppaṭiyāyiṉum kaliṅkatēcam jayikkappaṭṭatu; naṭantupōṉa kāriyam avaṉāl māṟṟakkūṭiyatā yirukkavillai. putitāka jayikkappaṭṭa jaṉaṅkaḷaip pātukāppataṟkum avarkaḷiṉ arukāmaiyiluḷḷa kāṭṭu jātiyāraic camrakṣaṇai ceyvataṟkum atikārikaḷukku vicēṣa eccarikkai ceyyappaṭṭatu. kaliṅka tēcattu atikārikaḷukku iṭappaṭṭirukkum kaṭṭaḷai iṉṉateṉṟu kaliṅka cāsaṉaṅkaḷ eṉṟa likitaṅkaḷiliruntu nām aṟintu koḷḷalām.

acōkaṉ muṭicūṭiya oṉpatāmāṇṭu mutal avaṉukkup ﻿

pautta matat

tiṉ sparcam

﻿pautta tarmattil pakticrattai atikarittukkoṇṭē vantatu. im māṟutalukkuk kaliṅka yuttattiṉ koṭumai maṭṭumalla kāraṇameṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. upakuptaṉ eṉṟa tuṟavi aracaṉukkup puttariṉ cuvicēṣattai upatēcittāreṉṟu vaṭa intiya aitihyaṅkaḷ collukiṉṟaṉa. cukanta vastukkaḷ ceyyum kuptaṉ makaṉ upakupta ṉeṉṟu ivarpeyar viritturaikkappaṭukiṟatu. ittuṟavi vivēkamum paricuttamum niṟaintiruntāreṉṟum puttarukkuppiṉ pautta caṅkattiṉ talaimai stāṉattai vakitta ācāriyaruḷ aintāvatāka niṉṟāreṉṟum, vekunāḷ acōkaṉukkuk kuruvākavum tuṇaivarākavum vāḻntu vantāreṉṟum collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki. pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiyāvil cañcārañceyta yuvaṉcuvaṅ eṉṟa cīṉa yāttirikaṉ ittuṟaviyiṉ ñāpakac ciṉṉaṅkaḷākak kaṭṭap paṭṭa vihāraṅkaḷ hintu tēcattilum vaṭa maturaiyilum ceḻittiruntaṉa veṉṟu kūṟukiṟāṉ. cilōṉ aitihyattil ivar peyar mōkalī puttira tissaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.

acōkaṉ pautta tarmattil īṭupaṭṭu mutalil upāsakaṉ eṉṟa nilaimaiyaik kaikkoṇṭāṉ. upāsakaṉ eṉpatu illaṟattil oḻukum pauttarukkup peyar. iraṇṭarai varuṣaṅkaḷukkuppiṉ, atāvatu aracaṉ muṭicūṭiya paṉṉiraṇṭām varuṣattil, avaṉ kāṭṭiya ūkkam taṉakkē tiruptikaramāyirukkavillai. muṉṉilum atika ūkkattuṭaṉ tarmattait taḻuviyoḻuka vēṇṭumeṉṟu avaṉ niccayittāṉ. ippaṭi oru varuṣamāṉa piṉ aracaṉ pautta saṅkattaiyē avalampittāṉ. itu nikaḻntatu aracaṉuṭaiya patiṉmūṉṟām paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil, ivvaruṣamum itaṟku muṉṉum piṉṉumuḷḷa varuṣaṅkaḷum acōkaṉiṉ - jīviya caritaiyil mikavum mukkiyamāṉavai. avaṉuṭaiya ellāc cīrtiruttaṅkaḷum naṉ muyaṟcikaḷum ivvāṇṭukaḷil ārampikkappaṭṭaṉa. araṇmaṉaic camaiyal aṟaiyil māmica vakai tavirkkappaṭṭatu jīva imsaiyum pirāṇivataiyum rājat turōkamākak karutappaṭumeṉṟu jaṉaṅkaḷ eccarikkai ceyyappaṭṭaṉar. taṉatu tarmattiṉ koḷkaikaḷaik kaṟkaḷilum stampaṅkaḷilum eḻuti avaṟṟai ellōrukkum teriyappaṭuttavēṇṭumeṉṟu aracaṉ niccayittāṉ. vēṭṭaiyāṭutal mutaliya paḻaiya poḻutu pōkkukaḷ tavirkkappaṭṭaṉa. ataṟkup patilāka avaṉ puṇṇiya stala yāttiraikaḷ ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. āṅkāṅkuḷḷa ñāṉacirēṣṭarkaḷait taricippatum, tarmattaip pōtippatum, tarma vivātaṅkaḷai naṭattuvatumē aracaṉuṭaiya poḻutupōkkāyiṟṟu, aracaṉuṭaiya vāḻnāṭkaḷil ippaṭippaṭṭa puṉita yāttiraikaḷ pala ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.

ivaṟṟil piratāṉamāṉatu acōkaṉiṉ irupattōrāvatu ﻿

⁠tīrtta

yāttiraikaḷ

﻿paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil naṭanta yāttirai. pāṭalipurattiliruntu puttariṉ cutēcamākiya kapilavastuvukkuc cellum pātaiyil ippōtu ēḻu stampaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. inta stampaṅkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟil acōka cāsaṉaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟiraṇṭil oruvita likitamumillai. inta stampaṅkaḷ muṉ collappaṭṭa tīrttayāttiraiyiṉ ñāpakac ciṉṉaṅkaḷeṉṟu nām ūkikkiṟōm. aitihyattiṉ paṭi acōkaṉ inta yāttiraiyil upakuptaṉaiyum aḻaittuc ceṉṟāṉ pōlum, avarkaḷ mutalil puttariṉ piṟappiṭamākiya lumpiṉi kirāmattait taricittārkaḷām ; piṉṉar, kapilavastu nakarattaiyum, puttar pūrṇañāṉam aṭainta iṭamākiya puttakayāvaiyum, ammakāṉ tamatu tarma cakkarattaic cuḻaṟṟa ārampitta sārnātattaiyum, avar kaṭaiciyāy parinirvāṇamaṭainta kucinakarattaiyum, avar tarmap piracāram ceytukoṇṭu palakālam vacittuvanta srīnāvastiyaiyum ivaikaḷallāmal vēṟu iraṇṭu kṣēttiraṅkaḷaiyum avarkaḷ taricittārkaḷām. puttariṉ piṟappiṭamākiya lumpiṉi taṟkālam rummiṉtēyi eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. inta kṣēttirattai acōkaṉ taricittatiṉ aṟikuṟiyāynāṭṭappaṭṭa stampam, veku kālamāka maṟaintu kiṭantatu; atu camīpakālattil tirumpavum uttāraṇam ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. acōkaṉ inta ūrilirunta jaṉaṅkaḷukku nilattīrvaiyaik kuṟaittāṉeṉṟu rummiṉ tēyi stampattiluḷḷa likitam namakkut terivikkiṉṟatu. acōkaṉ itē camayattil koṉākamaṉaṉ eṉṟa pūrvaputtariṉ kṣēttirattait taricaṉam ceytu, aṅkirunta stūpattaip putuppittu oru stampattaiyum nāṭṭiya ceytikaḷ aitihyattil kūṟappaṭavillaiyāyiṉum cilācāsaṉa paricōtaṉai mūlamāka nām terintukoḷḷukiṟōm. icceytikaḷ eḻutappaṭṭirukkum stampamum rummiṉtēyiṉ arukiliruntu namakku akappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivvivaraṅkaḷ acōkaṉatu tarmam ettaṉmaiyatu eṉṟu tīrmāṉikka upayōkamāṉavai.

nēppāḷattiṉ talainakaramākiya kātmāṇṭu allatu laḷitāpaṭṭaṇam acōkaṉāl stāpikkappaṭṭateṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. aṅku aracaṉ pala kōyilkaḷ kaṭṭiṉāṉeṉṟum aracaṉ makaḷ cārumati avaṉuṭaṉ aṅkuc ceṉṟu taṉ takappaṉāriṉ vēlaikku utaviyāyiruntu piṉ aṅkēyē nilaittiruntāḷeṉṟum aitihyattil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭṭaṇam rummiṉtēyiyiṉ arukiluḷḷatāl innakarattiṉ stāpaṉam rummiṉtēyi likitattil kuṟippiṭṭirukkum aracaṉiṉ irupattoṉṟām paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattilēyē naṭantirukkalām.

taṉatu paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu paṭṭāpiṣēka varuṣattil aracaṉ ﻿

⁠tuṟavi aracu

naṭattiya vintai

﻿pautta saṅkattaic cērntatāl avaṉ canniyāsi āṉāṉ. kaṭaicivaraiyum avaṉ inta āciramattiliruntu māṟavillai pōlum. ki. pi. ēḻāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil intiyāvil cañcaritta itsiṅ, tuṟaviyuṭaiyaṇinta acōkap piratimaiyaik kaṇṭatāka eḻutukiṟāṉ. āṉāl aitihyaṅkaḷilum cāsaṉaṅkaḷilum acōkaṉuṭaiya maṉaivimārkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. pikṣūvāṉapiṉ taṉ rājya kāriyaṅkaḷai acaṭṭaiceytāṉeṉṟum vaṭaaitihyattil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. talaiyaic ciraittuk , kāṣāyavastiramuṭutta aracaṉ eppaṭi ciṅkātaṉattil vīṟṟiruntāṉeṉṟum rājya kāriyaṅkaḷaik kavaṉittāṉeṉṟum namakkum caṅkai uṇṭākiṟatu. acōkaṉuṭaiya cāsaṉaṅkaḷ inta camcayattai muṟṟilum nivirtticeykiṉṟaṉa . “eṉatu kaṭamaiyāka ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa vēlaiyil nāṉ kāṭṭum jākkiratai eṉakku orupōtum tiruptikaramākiṟatillai. sarva jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ apivirttikkāka catā uḻaippatu eṉ kaṭamai.” “evviṭattilum jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻakkukaḷai vicāraṇai ceyya nāṉ tayārāyirukkiṟēṉ.” ippaṭip pēcum aracaṉ taṉ kaṭamaiyai acaṭṭai ceytāṉeṉṟu colvatai nampuvatu eppaṭi? tuṟavaṟamum ulaka naṉmaikkuriya piravirttiyum oṉṟukkoṉṟu muraṇallaveṉṟu pakavatkītaiyil vaṟpuṟuttappaṭum koḷkaiyē acōkaṉ koḷkaipōlum.



mēlum, cāsaṉaṅkaḷil aracaṉuṭaiya rāṇikaḷaiyum piḷḷaikaḷaiyumpaṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. tuṟaviyāṉa piṉ aracaṉ avarkaḷuṭaṉ vāḻavillaiyeṉṟu nām aṉumāṉañceytu koṇṭālum avarkaḷuṭaiya yōkakṣēmaṅkaḷai avaṉ kavaṉittuvantāṉeṉpatu teḷivu.[4] acōkaṉ kaṭaicivaraiyum taṉatu kaṭamaikaḷai niṟaivēṟṟuvatil kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāyiruntāṉeṉṟu nām kūṟuvatu mikaiyaṉṟu. ippaṭippaṭṭa ūkkamuṭaimai, aracarukku iṉṟiyamaiyātateṉṟu kauṭalyaṉuṭaiya arttacāstirattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl acōkaṉ iv viṣayattil tam muṉṉōrvaḻiyai aṉucarittu vantāṉaṉṟi vēṟillai.

saṅkattiṟ cērntapiṉ aracaṉ ūkkamuṭaimai muṉṉilum ﻿

tarmap piracā

ram ceytal

﻿atika vicālamaṭaintatu. turaittaṉak kāriyaṅkaḷil maṭṭum taṉatu ūkkattai niṟuttik koḷḷāmal ulakameṅkum tarmappiracārañceyyavēṇṭumeṉṟa avā. acōkaṉaip paṟṟiyatu. taṉatu rājyattiṉuḷḷum veḷiyilum tarmap piracāram naṭattiṉāṉ avaṉatu ēkātipattiyattukkuppaṭṭa yavaṉar, kāmpōjar, nāpakatēcattu nāpapantiyar, pōjar, pitēṉikar, āntirar, pulintar, rāṣṭirikar, kāntārar mutaliya jaṉaṅkaḷukkup pala tarma āciriyar aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar. anta jaṉaṅkaḷ acōkaṉ pōtaṉaiyai aṉpuṭaṉ kēṭṭārkaḷeṉṟu nām ūkikkalām.

tarma pōtaṉaikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭa piratēcaṅkaḷiṉ peyarum antanta iṭaṅkaḷukku aṉuppappaṭṭa āciriyariṉ peyarum cilōṉ aitihyattil[5] kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. kaṉṉiṅhām turai 1873-m ௵l sāñciyiṉ arukēyuḷḷa ērāḷamāṉa purātaṉa vastukkaḷaic cōtaṉai ceytapōtu pautta muṉivariṉ elumpukaḷ nikṣēpikkappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa. iraṇṭu pāttiraṅkaḷiṉ puṟattil “himavantap piratēcattiṉ ācāriyaṉākiya kāciyapa kōttiraṉ cāmpal” eṉṟum, “himavantap piratēcattiṉ ācāriyaṉākiya majjimaṉ cāmpal” eṉṟum likitaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. makāvamcattilum ippeyarkaḷuṭaiya ācāriyar acōkaṉ kālattil imayamalai allatu himavantap piratēcattil tarmattaip pōtittaṉareṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭiruppatum kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

suvarṇa pūmi, allatu sōvaṉa pūmi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum parmā tēcamum acōkaṉ tūtar mūlamāka pautta tarmattaic ceviyuṟṟateṉṟu iṅku collappaṭukiṟatu. acōkaṉuṭaiya rājyattukku uṭpaṭāta vēṟu palatēcattār acōkaṉ mūlamākavē pautta tarmattai aṟintaṉareṉpatu uṇmaiyē. teṉ intiyāvil cutantira aracarkaḷāyirunta cōḻar pāṇṭiyar, sattiyaputtirar, kēraḷa puttirar nāṭukaḷil tarma pōtaṉaikaḷum tarmīyamāṉa upakārac ceykaikaḷum naṭantuvantaṉa. cilōṉ tīviluḷḷa jaṉaṅkaḷum mutaṉ mutal puttariṉ santēcattaik kēṭṭatu acōkaṉ mūlamākavē. acōkaṉuṭaiya cakōtaraṉ makēntiraṉum, cakōtari saṅkamittiraiyum iṅku tarmap piracārañ ceyyumporuṭṭu aṉuppappaṭṭārkaḷeṉṟu itikāsaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ intiyāvilum ivar pautta tarmattaip paravac ceytaṉarpōlum. makēntira vihāram eṉṟa peyaruṭaiya maṭam oṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭil iruntatāka yuvaṉcuvaṅ kūṟukiṟāṉ. makāvamcattiṉpaṭi iv viruvarum acōkaṉuṭaiya makkaḷām.

ēṟakkuṟaiya acōkaṉ kālattatu eṉṟu aṉumāṉikkappaṭum ciṟpam oṉṟil cilōṉil pautta matattaip piracārañceyta viṣayam kuṟippiṭṭiruppatupōl tōṉṟukiṟatu. sāñci stūpattiṉ ōr vācaliṉ uttarakkallil uyaramāṉa ōr maṉitaṉ yāṉaiyiliruntu iṟaṅkuvataiyum pōtiliruṣattiṉ kiḷai ammarattiṉiṉṟu oṭikkappaṭṭu ammaṉitaṉiṭam koṭukkappaṭuvataiyum viḷakkikkāṭṭum ciṟpam oṉṟuṇṭu. pōtivirukṣattiṉ kiḷai cilōṉukku tēvar piriyaṉāṉa tissaṉ eṉṟa aracaṉ kālattil koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa ceyti cilōṉ itikāsaṅkaḷil mika vimaricaiyākak kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. muṉ kūṟappaṭṭa ciṟpam intac campavattiṉ aṟikuṟiyā yirukkalāmaṉṟō ?

acōkaṉāl tarmapracāram ceyyappaṭṭa nāṭukaḷuḷ mika ﻿

veku tūrat

tuḷḷa aintu

aracaruṭaṉ uṟavu.

﻿mukkiyamāṉavai aṟunūṟu yōjaṉaikkappāluḷḷa aintu aracariṉ nāṭukaḷ. āciyā airōppā āppirikkāk kaṇṭaṅkaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu cantikkum piratēcaṅkaḷai āṇṭuvanta aracar ivar. iṅku kirīkku jātiyāruṭaiya nākarikam mikuntiruntatu, alaksāntar eṉṟa māsiṭōṇiyā tēcattup parākrama vīraṉ ki. mu. 335-l taṉatu tikvijayattai ārampittu nūṟṟukkaṇakkāy rājyaṅkaḷai jayittu intiyāvukkum paṭaiyeṭuttuvanta vivaraṅkaḷ cātāraṇamāyt terintavaiyē. avvīraṉ parākkiramattāl ēṟpaṭṭa ēkātipatyam avaṉuṭaiya muṭivuṭaṉ cīrkulaintatu. alaksāntariṉ mukkiya pōrvīrarkaḷ appōtu anta ēkātipattiyattaip paṅkiṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar. patiṉmūṉṟām cāsaṉattil kūṟiyirukkum aintu aracarkaḷiṉ rājyaṅkaḷ alaksāntariṉ ēkātipattiyattiliruntu uṇṭāṉavai. cāsaṉattil aintu aracariṉ peyar maṭṭum kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivaruṭaiya kālamum tēcamum piṉ varumāṟu:-



* * *



aintu aracaruk⁠ ivvaracariṉ ivar āṇṭu⁠ kālam

kuc cāsaṉaṅka ⁠ ⁠ ⁠

ḷil vantuḷḷa airōppiyap peyar. vanta tēcam ki.mu.
